\displaystyle+e^{ik_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{i(j_{n_{h}}-1)k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{n_{h}}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\left(c_{j_{1},\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}}-1,\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle.

(S271)

Note that here we explicitly impose the constraint in the summation

\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}
, since it cannot be automatically guaranteed by the fermionic creation operator.
Moreover, we define

\displaystyle A_{1,-}

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}+1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\left(c_{j_{1}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S272a)

\displaystyle A_{1,+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}-1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\left(c_{j_{1}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S272b)

\displaystyle\cdots

\displaystyle A_{n_{h},-}

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{i(j_{n_{h}}+1)k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{n_{h}}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\left(c_{j_{1},\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}}+1,\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S272c)

\displaystyle A_{n_{h},+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{i(j_{n_{h}}-1)k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{n_{h}}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\left(c_{j_{1},\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}}-1,\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S272d)

and such that
A
defined in Eq. (
III.3.2
) is given by
A=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{h}}(A_{i,-}+A_{i,+})
.

The second part
B
of
\hat{H}|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle
is given by

Part B

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime}+1,\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}+c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime}-1,\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime}+1,\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\delta_{j^{\prime}+1,j_{a}}+c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime}-1,\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}\delta_{j^{\prime}-1,j_{a}}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{a}-1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{a}+1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}-1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}-1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1}-1,\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}+1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}+1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1}+1,\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+\,\cdots\,+e^{ik_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{i(j_{n_{h}}-1)k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{n_{h}}-1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{n_{h}},\downarrow}c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}}-1,\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{i(j_{n_{h}}+1)k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{n_{h}}+1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{n_{h}},\downarrow}c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}}+1,\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle.

(S273)

Moreover, we define

\displaystyle B_{1,-}

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}+1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}+1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1}+1,\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|1\cdots 1\rangle,

(S274a)

\displaystyle B_{1,+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}-1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}-1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1}-1,\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|1\cdots 1\rangle,

(S274b)

\displaystyle\cdots

\displaystyle B_{n_{h},-}

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{i(j_{n_{h}}+1)k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{n_{h}}+1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{n_{h}},\downarrow}c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}}+1,\uparrow}|1\cdots 1\rangle,

(S274c)

\displaystyle B_{n_{h},+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{i(j_{n_{h}}-1)k_{n_{h}}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{n_{h}}-1)k_{\alpha}}\left(c^{\dagger}_{j_{n_{h}},\downarrow}c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}}-1,\uparrow}|1\cdots 1\rangle,

(S274d)

such that part
B
defined in Eq. (
III.3.2
) is given by
B=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{h}}(B_{i,-}+B_{i,+})
.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\hat{H}|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{h}}(A_{i,-}+A_{i,+})+\sum_{i=1}^{n_{h}}(B_{i,-}+B_{i,+})=\sum_{i=1}^{n_{h}}((A_{i,-}+B_{i,-})+(A_{i,+}+B_{i,+})).

(S275)

We first consider
A_{1,-}+B_{1,-}
. We have

\displaystyle A_{1,-}+B_{1,-}

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}+1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}+1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left(c_{j_{1}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1}=0}^{N-2}e^{i(j_{1}+1)k_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}+1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{j_{1}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{iNk_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq N-1\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(N-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(N-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};N)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{N-1,\downarrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{0,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}-1\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1}-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1}-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1}-1,\downarrow}c_{j_{1}-1,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{j_{1},\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{iNk_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq N-1\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(N-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(N-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};0)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{N-1,\downarrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{0,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{N-1}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}-1\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}-1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1}-1,\downarrow}c_{j_{1}-1,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{j_{1},\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{iNk_{1}}

\displaystyle\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq N-1\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ik_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(0,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{-i(N-n_{h}-1)k_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(0,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};N-1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{N-1,\downarrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\left(c_{0,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S276)

where in the last equality we have used equalities in Eq. (
S269
). Therefore, when
e^{iNk_{1}}e^{ik_{\alpha}}=1
and
e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}=1
,

\displaystyle A_{1,-}+B_{1,-}=e^{-ik_{1}}|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle.

(S277)

Similarly, we can also prove that when
e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}=1
and
e^{iNk_{a}}e^{ik_{\alpha}}=1
for
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
, we have

\displaystyle A_{a,-}+B_{a,-}=e^{-ik_{a}}|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle.

(S278)

See Sec.
III.3.3
for the proof and further details.

We then consider
A_{1,+}+B_{1,+}
. We have

\displaystyle A_{1,+}+B_{1,+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}-1)k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}-1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left(c_{j_{1}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{N-1}e^{i(j_{1}-1)k_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}-1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1},\downarrow}c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{j_{1}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{-ik_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq 0\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(0,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(0,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};N-1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{0,\downarrow}c_{0,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{N-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1}=0}^{N-2}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}+1\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1}+1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1}+1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1}+1,\downarrow}c_{j_{1}+1,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{j_{1},\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{-ik_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq 0\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(0,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(0,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};N-1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{0,\downarrow}c_{0,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{N-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}\sum_{j_{1}=0}^{N-2}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq j_{1}+1\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j_{1}+1)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{1}+1,\downarrow}c_{j_{1}+1,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{j_{1},\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{ik_{1}}\sum_{j_{2},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{2}k_{2}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}e^{-ik_{1}}

\displaystyle\left((\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j^{\prime}=0\\
j^{\prime}\neq 0\end{subarray}}^{N-1}e^{-k\alpha}e^{ij_{\alpha}(N-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow})+e^{i(N-n_{h}-1)k_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(N-1,\cdots,j_{n_{h}};0)k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{0,\downarrow}c_{0,\uparrow}\right)\left(c_{N-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{2},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S279)

where in the last equality we have used equalities in Eq. (
S269
). Therefore, when
e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}=1
and
e^{iNk_{1}}e^{ik_{\alpha}}=1
,

\displaystyle A_{1,+}+B_{1,+}=e^{ik_{1}}|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle.

(S280)

Similarly, we can also prove that when
e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}=1
and
e^{iNk_{a}}e^{ik_{\alpha}}=1
for
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
, we have

\displaystyle A_{a,+}+B_{a,+}=e^{ik_{a}}|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle.

(S281)

See Sec.
III.3.3
for the proof and further details.

In conclusion, when

\displaystyle e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=1,

(S282)

\displaystyle e^{ik_{\alpha}}e^{iNk_{a}}

\displaystyle=1,\quad a=1,\cdots,n_{h},

(S283)

we have

\displaystyle\hat{H}|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle=\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}2\cos(k_{a})|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha}\rangle.

(S284)

Moreover,
k_{\alpha}
and
k_{a}
with
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle k_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N-n_{h}},\quad\mathrm{with}\quad m_{\downarrow}=0,\cdots,N-n_{h}-1,

(S285)

\displaystyle k_{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\pi m_{a}}{N}-\frac{k_{\alpha}}{N}=\frac{2\pi m_{a}}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-n_{h})},\quad a=1,\cdots,n_{h}.

(S286)

Since any two
k_{i}
and
k_{j}
are distinct,
0\leq m_{1}<m_{2}<\cdots<m_{n_{h}}\leq N-1
.

III.3.3
Any number of spin-down fermions

In this section, we derive the eigenstates and the energy spectrum for arbitrary numbers of holes and spin-down fermions.

In the sector with
n_{h}
holes and
n_{\downarrow}
spin-down fermions, the Hilbert-space dimension is
\binom{N}{n_{h}}\binom{N-n_{h}}{n_{\downarrow}}
.
We assume that
N-n_{h}
is a prime number. After removing the
n_{h}
sites occupied by holes, the remaining system forms a
(N-n_{h})
-site chain. In this chain there are
\binom{N-n_{h}}{n_{\downarrow}}

possible spin configurations and these
\binom{N-n_{h}}{n_{\downarrow}}
spin configurations form
\frac{\binom{N-n_{h}}{n_{\downarrow}}}{N-n_{h}}

orbits under translation, and each orbit has length
N-n_{h}
. See the proof in Sec.
III.1.4
.

For a given spin configuration, we denote by
x_{i}
with
i=1,\cdots,n_{\downarrow}
the position of
i
-th spin-down fermion in the
(N-n_{h})
-chain and denote by
d_{i}=x_{i+1}-x_{i}
the distance between
(i+1)
-th and
i
-th spin-down fermions in the
(N-n_{h})
-site chain. Note that
d_{i}
is defined modulo
N-n_{h}
. Here, we show an example. When
N=9
,
n_{h}=2
,
n_{\downarrow}=3
, in the remaining
7
-site chain, there are
35
possible spin configurations:

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{-1,-1,-1,+1,+1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,1,5)

(S287)

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,-1,-1,+1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,1,5)

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,-1,-1,-1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,1,5)

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{4}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,+1,-1,-1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,1,5)

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{5}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,+1,+1,-1,-1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,1,5)

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{6}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,+1,+1,+1,-1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(5,1,1)

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{7}

\displaystyle=\{-1,-1,+1,+1,+1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,5,1)

\displaystyle s^{(2)}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{-1,-1,+1,-1,+1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,2,4)

(S288)

\displaystyle s^{(2)}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,-1,+1,-1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,2,4)

\displaystyle s^{(2)}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,-1,-1,+1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,2,4)

\displaystyle s^{(2)}_{4}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,+1,-1,-1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,2,4)

\displaystyle s^{(2)}_{5}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,+1,+1,-1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(4,1,2)

\displaystyle s^{(2)}_{6}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,+1,+1,+1,-1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(4,1,2)

\displaystyle s^{(2)}_{7}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,-1,+1,+1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,4,1)

\displaystyle s^{(3)}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{-1,-1,+1,+1,-1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,3,3)

(S289)

\displaystyle s^{(3)}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,-1,+1,+1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,3,3)

\displaystyle s^{(3)}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,-1,-1,+1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,3,3)

\displaystyle s^{(3)}_{4}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,+1,-1,-1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(3,1,3)

\displaystyle s^{(3)}_{5}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,+1,+1,-1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(3,1,3)

\displaystyle s^{(3)}_{6}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,-1,+1,+1,-1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(3,1,3)

\displaystyle s^{(3)}_{7}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,+1,-1,+1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(3,3,1)

\displaystyle s^{(4)}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{-1,-1,+1,+1,+1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,4,2)

(S290)

\displaystyle s^{(4)}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,-1,+1,+1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(1,4,2)

\displaystyle s^{(4)}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,-1,-1,+1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,1,4)

\displaystyle s^{(4)}_{4}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,+1,-1,-1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,1,4)

\displaystyle s^{(4)}_{5}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,-1,+1,-1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,1,4)

\displaystyle s^{(4)}_{6}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,+1,-1,+1,-1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,1,4)

\displaystyle s^{(4)}_{7}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,+1,+1,-1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(4,2,1)

\displaystyle s^{(5)}_{1}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,-1,+1,-1,+1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,2,3)

(S291)

\displaystyle s^{(5)}_{2}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,+1,-1,+1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,2,3)

\displaystyle s^{(5)}_{3}

\displaystyle=\{+1,+1,-1,+1,-1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,2,3)

\displaystyle s^{(5)}_{4}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,+1,-1,+1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(3,2,2)

\displaystyle s^{(5)}_{5}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,+1,+1,-1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(3,2,2)

\displaystyle s^{(5)}_{6}

\displaystyle=\{-1,+1,-1,+1,+1,-1,+1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,3,2)

\displaystyle s^{(5)}_{7}

\displaystyle=\{+1,-1,+1,-1,+1,+1,-1\};\quad(d_{1},d_{2},d_{3})=(2,3,2)

If
(d_{1},d_{2},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}})
and
(d^{\prime}_{1},d^{\prime}_{2},\cdots,d^{\prime}_{n_{\downarrow}})
are related by a cyclic permutation, the corresponding two spin configurations belong to the same orbit. For example, the
7
spin configurations shown in Eq. (
S287
) belong to the same orbit

\displaystyle s^{(1)}_{1}\xrightarrow{\hat{T}}s^{(1)}_{2}\xrightarrow{\hat{T}}s^{(1)}_{3}\xrightarrow{\hat{T}}s^{(1)}_{4}\xrightarrow{\hat{T}}s^{(1)}_{5}\xrightarrow{\hat{T}}s^{(1)}_{6}\xrightarrow{\hat{T}}s^{(1)}_{7}\xrightarrow{\hat{T}}s^{(1)}_{1}.

(S292)

The spin configurations shown in Eqs. (
S288
)–(
S291
) form four additional orbits.

For each orbit, we choose a representative spin configuration
with
(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}})
and designate the left-most
spin-down fermion as the first spin-down fermion.
Once the representative configuration is fixed, the other spin
configurations in the orbit are obtained by applying the translation
operator to it. In these translated configurations, the first
spin-down fermion is defined as the translation of the first
spin-down fermion in the representative configuration.

Then we define the following state

\displaystyle|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}})\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}c^{\dagger}_{j^{\prime},\downarrow}c_{j^{\prime},\uparrow}

\displaystyle c^{\dagger}_{r(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}))_{2},\downarrow}c_{r(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}))_{2},\uparrow}\cdots

\displaystyle c^{\dagger}_{r(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}))_{n_{\downarrow}},\downarrow}c_{r(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}))_{n_{\downarrow}},\uparrow}

\displaystyle c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{1}k_{1}}\cdots e^{ij_{n_{h}}k_{n_{h}}}

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}))_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}))_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle.

(S293)

For simplicity, we first introduce the shorthands used in the following derivation

\displaystyle\mathbf{k}

\displaystyle\equiv(k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}}),

(S294a)

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}

\displaystyle\equiv(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}),

(S294b)

\displaystyle\mathbf{j}

\displaystyle\equiv(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}),

(S294c)

\displaystyle\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)}

\displaystyle\equiv(j_{1},\cdots,j_{a-1},j_{a}+1,j_{a+1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}),

(S294d)

\displaystyle\mathbf{j}^{(a-1)}

\displaystyle\equiv(j_{1},\cdots,j_{a-1},j_{a}-1,j_{a+1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}),

(S294e)

\displaystyle\mathbf{j}_{a}

\displaystyle\equiv(j_{1},\cdots,j_{a-1},j_{a+1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}),

(S294f)

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{j}}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1},

(S294g)

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{j}_{a}}

\displaystyle\equiv\sum_{j_{1},\cdots,j_{a-1},\,j_{a+1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}=0}^{N-1}.

(S294h)

Since
\textbf{j}_{a}
has excluded
j_{a}
, we can also express
j
as
(\textbf{j}_{a},j_{a})
.

Here
r(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}};j^{\prime},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}}))_{i}=r(\textbf{j},j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

with
i=1,\cdots,n_{\downarrow}
represents the position of the
i
-th spin-down fermion in the chain of sites
N
, when the
n_{h}
holes are located at the sites
\textbf{j}=(j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}})
and the first spin-down fermion is located at the site
j^{\prime}
and the spin configuration is determined by
\textbf{d}=(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}})
.
r(\textbf{j},j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}=j_{\alpha}^{-1}\!\left(\textbf{j};\,j_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\;\;(\mathrm{mod}\;N-n_{h})\right).

(S295)

See Eq. (
S268
) for the definition of
j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})
. The function
j_{\alpha}^{-1}
denotes the inverse of
j_{\alpha}
, i.e.,
j_{\alpha}^{-1}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})
gives the position of the spin-down fermion in the
N
-site chain when its position in the
(N-n_{h})
-site chain is
j^{\prime}
, with the hole positions specified by
\mathbf{j}
. It is defined as

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}^{-1}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})=\tilde{j}+\sum_{m=1}^{n_{h}}\Theta\!\bigl(j^{\prime}-(\tilde{j}_{m}-m+\frac{1}{2})\bigr),

(S296)

where
\tilde{j}_{1}<\tilde{j}_{2}<\cdots<\tilde{j}_{n_{h}}

be the hole positions obtained by sorting the set
\{j_{1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}}\}
in ascending order.

We further define

l(\textbf{j};j^{\prime\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

to denote the position of the first spin-down fermion in the original

N
-site chain when the
i
-th spin-down fermion is located at site
j^{\prime\prime}
.
Since the position of the
i
-th spin-down fermion in the reduced

(N-n_{h})
-site chain differs from that of the first spin-down fermion by

\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}
, we have

\displaystyle l(\textbf{j};j^{\prime\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}=j_{\alpha}^{-1}\!\left(\textbf{j};\,j_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime\prime})-\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}\right).

(S297)

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};\,l(\textbf{j};j^{\prime\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\,\textbf{d})_{i}=j^{\prime\prime}.

(S298)

With the shorthand notation defined above (Eqs. (
S294a
)-(
S294h
)), the state
|k_{1},\cdots,k_{n_{h}},k_{\alpha},(d_{1},\cdots,d_{n_{\downarrow}})\rangle
can be rewritten as

\displaystyle|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle=\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle.

(S299)

Under the action of Hamiltonian
\hat{H}
,

\displaystyle\hat{H}|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}(c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}+c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow})+\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}(c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}+1,\downarrow}+c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow})c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\downarrow}\right)

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}(A_{a,-}+A_{a,+}+B_{a,-}+B_{a,+}).

(S300)

The
A_{a,-}
with
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) is given by

\displaystyle A_{a,-}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S301)

where we have used
e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}=e^{-ik_{a}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}
since
\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}=(j_{1},\cdots,j_{a-1},j_{a}+1,j_{a+1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}})
. Note that in the summation over
j^{\prime}
in
A_{a,-}
, we impose the constraints for any
i=1,\cdots,n_{\downarrow}

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle\neq j_{b},\quad b=1,\cdots,n_{h},

(S302)

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle\neq j_{a}+1.

Apart from the constraint
r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}\neq j_{a}
, the remaining constraints are automatically satisfied by the fermionic creation operators.

The
A_{a,+}
with
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) is given by

\displaystyle A_{a,+}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S303)

where we have used
e^{i\text{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}=e^{ik_{a}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}
since
\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}=(j_{1},\cdots,j_{a-1},j_{a}-1,j_{a-1},\cdots,j_{n_{h}})
. Note that in the summation over
j^{\prime}
in
A_{a,+}
, we impose the constraints for any
i=1,\cdots,n_{\downarrow}

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle\neq j_{b},\quad b=1,\cdots,n_{h},

(S304)

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle\neq j_{a}-1.

Apart from the constraint
r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}\neq j_{a}
, the remaining constraints are automatically satisfied due to the fermion creation operators.

The
B_{a,-}
with
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) is given by

\displaystyle B_{a,-}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\downarrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\uparrow}\delta_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,j_{a}}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle.

(S305)

Note that these terms correspond to the cases where

r(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i}=j_{a}
for
i=1,\cdots,n_{\downarrow}
,
which are excluded in
A_{a,-}
, as shown in Eq. (
S302
).

Similarly, by replacing
\textbf{j}^{a+1}
with
\textbf{j}^{a-1}
and
j_{a}+1
with
j_{a}-1
, the
B_{a,+}
with
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) is given by

\displaystyle B_{a,+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle.

(S306)

Note that these terms correspond to the cases where
r(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i}=j_{a}
for
i=1,\cdots,n_{\downarrow}
, which have been excluded in
A_{a,+}
, as shown in Eq. (
S304
).

Next, we first consider
A_{a,-}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
).

\displaystyle A_{a,-}

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j_{a}=0}^{N-2}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},N)\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{0,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j_{a}=0}^{N-2}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ik_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{iNk_{a}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},0)\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{0,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S307)

where in the third equality we have used

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime}),

(S308a)

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}.

(S308b)

and

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})+1,

(S309a)

\displaystyle r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i}.

(S309b)

(the proof is shown in Sec.
III.3.4
).

We then consider
B_{a,-}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
).

\displaystyle B_{a,-}

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j_{a}=0}^{N-2}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},N)\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{N-1,\downarrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{0,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j_{a}=0}^{N-2}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{-ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{iNk_{a}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},0)\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{-i(N-n_{h}-1)k_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{N-1,\downarrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{0,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S310)

where in the third equality we have used

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b})

(S311)

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i}

(S312)

(the proof is shown in Sec.
III.3.4
) and

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b})-(N-n_{h}-1)

(S313)

\displaystyle r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i}

(S314)

(the proof is shown in Sec.
III.3.4
).

Combining
A_{a,-}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) and
B_{a,-}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
), and imposing the conditions

e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}=1
and
e^{iNk_{a}}e^{ik_{\alpha}}=1
for
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}\left(A_{a,-}+B_{a,-}\right)=\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{-ik_{a}}\,|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle.

(S315)

Next, we consider
A_{a,+}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
).

\displaystyle A_{a,+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j_{a}=1}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},-1)\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j_{a}=1}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{-ik_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{-iNk_{a}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},N-1)\cdot\textbf{k}}\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S316)

where, in the third equality, we have used

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a-1)};j^{\prime}),

(S317)

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r(\mathbf{j}^{(a-1)};j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i},

(S318)

(the proof is the same as that of
Eqs. (
S308a
) and (
S308b
) by replacing
\textit{j}^{(a+1)}
with
\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}
), and

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})-1,

(S319)

\displaystyle r((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i},

(S320)

which are the same as
Eqs. (
S309a
) and (
S309b
).

Finally, we consider
B_{a,+}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
).

\displaystyle B_{a,+}

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j_{a}=1}^{N-1}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},-1)\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{0,\downarrow}c_{0,\uparrow}

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},0);l((\textbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{N,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle=e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{j_{a}=1}^{N-1}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{i\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\downarrow}c_{j_{a},\uparrow}\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a-1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle

\displaystyle+e^{ik_{a}}\sum_{\textbf{j}_{a}}\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}e^{-iNk_{a}}e^{i(\textbf{j}_{a},N-1)\cdot\textbf{k}}e^{i(N-n_{h}-1)k_{\alpha}}e^{ij_{\alpha}((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b})k_{\alpha}}c^{\dagger}_{0,\downarrow}c_{0,\uparrow}

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{\begin{subarray}{c}i=1\\
i\neq b\end{subarray}}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\textbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)c_{j_{1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{a-1},\uparrow}c_{N-1,\uparrow}c_{j_{a+1},\uparrow}\cdots c_{j_{n_{h}},\uparrow}|\mathrm{FM}\rangle,

(S321)

where, in the third equality, we have used

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}-1,\mathbf{d})_{b})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a-1)};l(\mathbf{j}^{(a-1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{b}),

(S322)

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j};l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}-1,\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r(\mathbf{j}^{(a-1)};l(\mathbf{j}^{(a-1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i},

(S323)

(the proof is the same as that of
Eqs. (
S311
) and (
S312
) by replacing
\textbf{j}^{(a+1)}
with
\textbf{j}^{(a-1)}
),
and

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b})+(N-n_{h}-1),

(S324)

\displaystyle r((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i},

(S325)

which are the same as those in
Eqs. (
S313
) and (
S314
).

Combining
A_{a,+}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) and
B_{a,+}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
), and imposing the conditions
e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}=1
and
e^{iNk_{a}}e^{ik_{\alpha}}=1
for
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}(A_{a,+}+B_{a,+})=\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{ik_{a}}|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle.

(S326)

In conclusion, when

\displaystyle e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=1,

(S327)

\displaystyle e^{ik_{\alpha}}e^{iNk_{a}}

\displaystyle=1,\quad a=1,\cdots,n_{h},

(S328)

we have

\displaystyle\hat{H}|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle=\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}2\cos(k_{a})|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle.

(S329)

Moreover,
k_{\alpha}
and
k_{a}
with
a=1,\cdots,n_{h}
can be expressed as

\displaystyle k_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N-n_{h}},\quad\mathrm{with}\quad m_{\downarrow}=0,\cdots,N-n_{h}-1,

(S330)

\displaystyle k_{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\pi m_{a}}{N}-\frac{k_{\alpha}}{N}=\frac{2\pi m_{a}}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-n_{h})},\quad a=1,\cdots,n_{h}.

(S331)

Since any two
k_{i}
and
k_{j}
(
i,j=1,\cdots,n_{h}
) are distinct when
i\neq j
,
0\leq m_{1}<m_{2}<\cdots<m_{n_{h}}\leq N-1
. Physically, the condition
e^{i(N-n_{h})k_{\alpha}}=1
arises because, after removing the
n_{h}
sites occupied by the holes, the spin configurations with the same
\mathbf{d}
form an orbit on the
(N-n_{h})
-site chain.

Since the eigenenergy does not depend on the spin configuration
\mathbf{d}
, for each energy level

\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}2\cos(k_{a})
the degeneracy is

\binom{N-n_{h}}{n_{\downarrow}}/(N-n_{h})
, i.e., the number of orbits formed by the spin configurations.

Next, we prove that
|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle
is an eigenstate of current operator
\hat{J}
with eigenvalue
\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}2\sin(k_{a})|\textbf{j},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle
. Under the action of current operator
\hat{J}
,

\displaystyle\hat{J}|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle

\displaystyle=i\sum_{\textbf{j}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=0}^{N-1}e^{ij_{\alpha}(\textbf{j};j^{\prime})k_{\alpha}}e^{i\textbf{j}\cdot\textbf{k}}

(S332)

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}(-c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}+c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow})+\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}(c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}+1,\downarrow}-c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow})c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\downarrow}\right)

\displaystyle\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{i},\uparrow}\right)\left(\prod_{i=1}^{n_{h}}c_{j_{i},\uparrow}\right)|\mathrm{FM}\rangle.

Comparing with Eq. (
III.3.3
), the only difference is that
-\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}-\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\downarrow}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) becomes
-i\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}-i\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\downarrow}
and
-\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}-1,\uparrow}-\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}+1,\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\downarrow}
in Eq. (
III.3.3
) becomes
i\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}c^{\dagger}_{j_{a},\uparrow}c_{j_{a}+1,\uparrow}+i\sum_{b=1}^{n_{\downarrow}}c^{\dagger}_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b}-1,\downarrow}c_{r(\textbf{j};j^{\prime},\textbf{d})_{b},\downarrow}
. From the derivation for Eq. (
III.3.3
), we know the former contributes
\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{-ik_{a}}|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle
and the latter contributes
\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{ik_{a}}|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\hat{J}|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle

\displaystyle=(i\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{-ik_{a}}-i\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}e^{ik_{a}})|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle

\displaystyle=\sum_{a=1}^{n_{h}}2\sin(k_{a})|\textbf{k},k_{\alpha},\textbf{d}\rangle.

(S333)

III.3.4
Proof of equalities used in the eigenstate construction

In this section, we present the proofs of the equalities given in
Eqs. (
S308a
), (
S308b
),
(
S309a
), (
S309b
),
(
S311
), (
S312
),
(
S313
), and (
S314
).

Proof of the identities given in Eqs. (
S308a
), (
S308b
) used in
A_{a,-}
:

We first present the proof of equalities given in
Eqs. (
S308a
), (
S308b
), under the constraints given in Eq. (
S302
).

Since the two hole configurations
\mathbf{j}
and
\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)}
differ
only in that the removed site
j_{a}
is replaced by
j_{a}+1
,
by definition shown in Eq. (
S268
), we have

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})-j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime})=-\Theta(j^{\prime}-j_{a})+\Theta(j^{\prime}-(j_{a}+1)).

(S334)

For
j^{\prime}\neq j_{a},j_{a}+1
, the right-hand side vanishes. Therefore,

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime}),

(S335)

which proves Eq. (
S308a
).

Next, when the first spin-down fermion is located at site
j^{\prime}
, the reduced-chain coordinate of the

i
-th spin-down fermion is given by

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S336)

Since Eq. (
S308a
) shows that the reduced-chain
coordinate of the first spin-down fermion is the same for
\mathbf{j}
and

\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)}
, the reduced-chain coordinate of the
i
-th spin-down
fermion is also the same in the two configurations. Furthermore, due to constraints in Eq. (
S302
), no spin-down fermion is located at sites
j_{a}
or
j_{a}+1
,
moving the hole from
j_{a}
to
j_{a}+1
does not change the
physical occupied site corresponding to this reduced-chain coordinate. Hence,

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i}=r(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i},

(S337)

which proves Eq. (
S308b
).

Proof of the identities given in Eqs. (
S309a
), (
S309b
) used in
A_{a,-}
:

We then present the proof of equalities given in
Eqs. (
S309a
), (
S309b
), under the constraints given in Eq. (
S302
). Thus, there is no spin-down fermion located at sites
0
or
N-1
.

By definition shown in Eq. (
S268
), we obtain

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})-j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})=\Theta(j^{\prime}-0)-\Theta(j^{\prime}-(N-1)).

(S338)

For
j^{\prime}\neq 0,N-1
, one has

\displaystyle\Theta(j^{\prime})=1,\qquad\Theta(j^{\prime}-(N-1))=0,

(S339)

and therefore

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})=j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})+1,

(S340)

which proves Eq. (
S309a
).

Due to Eq. (
S309a
), we have

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}=j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})+1+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S341)

Therefore, the reduced-chain coordinate of the
i
-th spin-down fermion in the
configuration
(\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1)
is

\displaystyle y_{i}^{(N-1)}

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime})+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}

\displaystyle=\left[j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})+1\right]+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}

\displaystyle=y_{i}^{(0)}+1\pmod{N-n_{h}},

(S342)

where
y^{(0)}_{i}
is the reduced-chain coordinate of the
i
-th spin-down fermion in the configuration
(\textbf{j}_{a},0)
,

\displaystyle y_{i}^{(0)}=j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime})+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S343)

Therefore, we obtain (see the definition of
r(\cdot)
function in Eq. (
S295
))

\displaystyle r((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}^{-1}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);y_{i}^{(N-1)})

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}^{-1}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);y_{i}^{(N-1)}-1)

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}^{-1}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);y_{i}^{(0)})

\displaystyle=r((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);j^{\prime},\mathbf{d})_{i}.

(S344)

This proves Eq. (
S309b
).

Proof of the identities given in Eqs. (
S311
), (
S312
) used in
B_{a,-}
:

We then present the proof of equalities given in
Eqs. (
S311
), (
S312
).

For

\displaystyle l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{b},\qquad l(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{b},

(S345)

when
b=1
, we have

\displaystyle l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{1}

\displaystyle=j_{a}+1,

(S346)

\displaystyle l(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{1}

\displaystyle=j_{a}.

(S347)

Therefore, proving Eq. (
S311
) is equivalent to proving

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1)-j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a})=0.

(S348)

Using the definition of
j_{\alpha}
shown in Eq. (
S268
), we have

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1)-j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a})

\displaystyle=\left[j_{a}+1-\Theta\!\bigl((j_{a}+1)-j_{a}\bigr)\right]-\left[j_{a}-\Theta\!\bigl(j_{a}-(j_{a}+1)\bigr)\right]

\displaystyle=\left(j_{a}+1-1\right)-\left(j_{a}-0\right)

\displaystyle=0.

(S349)

When
b\neq 1
,
let

\displaystyle x_{0}:=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1)=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a}),

(S350)

which is the common reduced-chain coordinate of the occupied sites

j_{a}+1
in the configuration
\mathbf{j}
and
j_{a}
in the configuration

\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)}
. By definition, if the
b
-th spin-down fermion is fixed at reduced-chain
coordinate
x_{0}
, then the first spin-down fermion must be located at

\displaystyle x_{0}-\sum_{\ell=1}^{b-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S351)

Hence,

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}\!\left(\mathbf{j};l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)=j_{\alpha}\!\left(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)=x_{0}-\sum_{\ell=1}^{b-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S352)

This proves Eq. (
S311
).

Next, we prove Eq. (
S312
),

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i}.

Note that we require
b\neq i
.
When
b=1
,

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j};l(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{1},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r(\textbf{j};j_{a}+1,\textbf{d})_{i}

(S353)

\displaystyle r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{b},\textbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=r(\textbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\textbf{d})_{i}

(S354)

Therefore, proving Eq. (
S312
) reduces to proving

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{i}=r(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{i},\qquad i\neq 1.

(S355)

The definition of
r(\cdot)
function is shown in Eq. (
S295
).
Applying this formula to the two sides of Eq. (
S355
),
we obtain

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{j};\,j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1)+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}\right),

(S356)

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{i}

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};\,j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a})+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}\right).

(S357)

From Eq. (
S311
), specialized to
b=1
, we already know
that

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1)=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a}).

(S358)

Hence the reduced-chain coordinates appearing in
Eqs. (
S356
) and (
S357
) differ only through the
inverse maps
j_{\alpha}^{-1}(\mathbf{j};\cdot)
and

j_{\alpha}^{-1}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};\cdot)
.

Now let

\displaystyle x_{0}:=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1)=j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a}),

(S359)

which is precisely the reduced-chain coordinate of the occupied site
j_{a}+1
in
the configuration
\mathbf{j}
and of the occupied site
j_{a}
in the
configuration
\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)}
.

For
i\neq 1
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}>0,

(S360)

and therefore

\displaystyle x_{0}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\not\equiv x_{0}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S361)

That is, the reduced-chain coordinate of the
i
-th spin-down fermion is not
the special coordinate
x_{0}
at which the inverse maps for
\mathbf{j}
and

\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)}
differ. Therefore,

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{j};\,x_{0}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\right)=j_{\alpha}^{-1}\!\left(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};\,x_{0}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\right),

(S362)

which immediately implies

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{i}=r(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{i},\qquad i\neq 1.

(S363)

For
b\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{b}=l(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{b}.

(S364)

Moreover, Eq. (
S308a
) gives

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}\!\left(\mathbf{j};l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)=j_{\alpha}\!\left(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{b}\right).

(S365)

Therefore the reduced-chain coordinate of the
i
-th spin-down fermion is the
same in the two descriptions. Since
i\neq b
, the
i
-th spin-down fermion is
not the one occupying the special sites
j_{a}+1
in
\mathbf{j}
and
j_{a}
in

\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)}
. Hence the corresponding physical site is unchanged after
mapping back to the original chain, and we obtain

\displaystyle r(\mathbf{j};l(\mathbf{j};j_{a}+1,\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i}=r(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};l(\mathbf{j}^{(a+1)};j_{a},\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i},\qquad i\neq b.

(S366)

This completes the proof of Eq. (
S312
).

Proof of the identities given in Eqs. (
S313
), (
S314
) used in
B_{a,-}
:

We then present the proof of equalities given in
Eqs. (
S313
), (
S314
).

We now prove Eq. (
S313
)

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)=j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)-(N-n_{h}-1).

Recall that for a given hole configuration
\mathbf{j}
and reference site
j^{\prime}
, the
position of the
i
-th spin-down fermion in the reduced
(N-n_{h})
-site chain is

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}(\mathbf{j};j^{\prime})+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S367)

Equivalently, if the
b
-th spin-down fermion is fixed at reduced-chain coordinate

x
, then the first spin-down fermion must be located at

\displaystyle x-\sum_{\ell=1}^{b-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S368)

We first consider the hole configuration
(\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1)
. In this case, the
site
0
is occupied and is the leftmost site in the reduced chain. Therefore,
its reduced-chain coordinate is

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0)=0.

(S369)

By definition,

\displaystyle l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b}

(S370)

is the position of the first spin-down fermion in the original
N
-site chain
such that the
b
-th spin-down fermion is located at site
0
. Since the latter
has reduced-chain coordinate
0
, Eq. (
S368
)
gives

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)=-\sum_{\ell=1}^{b-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S371)

Next we consider the hole configuration
(\mathbf{j}_{a},0)
. In this case, the
site
N-1
is occupied and is the rightmost site in the reduced chain. Hence its
reduced-chain coordinate is

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1)=N-n_{h}-1.

(S372)

Similarly, by definition,

\displaystyle l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b}

(S373)

is the position of the first spin-down fermion in the original chain such that
the
b
-th spin-down fermion is located at site
N-1
. Since the latter has
reduced-chain coordinate
N-n_{h}-1
, Eq. (
S368
)
implies

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)=N-n_{h}-1-\sum_{\ell=1}^{b-1}d_{\ell}\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S374)

Comparing Eqs. (
S371
) and
(
S374
), we obtain

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\ell=1}^{b-1}d_{\ell}

\displaystyle=\left(N-n_{h}-1-\sum_{\ell=1}^{b-1}d_{\ell}\right)-(N-n_{h}-1)

\displaystyle=j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right)-(N-n_{h}-1),

(S375)

which proves Eq. (
S313
).

Next, we prove Eq. (
S314
).
Let

\displaystyle x_{b}^{(L)}

\displaystyle:=j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right),

(S376)

\displaystyle x_{b}^{(R)}

\displaystyle:=j_{\alpha}\!\left((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b}\right).

(S377)

From Eq. (
S313
), we have

\displaystyle x_{b}^{(L)}=x_{b}^{(R)}-(N-n_{h}-1).

(S378)

Hence the reduced-chain coordinates of the
i
-th spin-down fermion satisfy

\displaystyle x_{b}^{(L)}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}=x_{b}^{(R)}+\sum_{\ell=1}^{i-1}d_{\ell}+1\pmod{N-n_{h}}.

(S379)

Since the hole configurations
(\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1)
and
(\mathbf{j}_{a},0)
differ
only by a cyclic relabeling of the reduced chain by one site, their inverse maps
satisfy

\displaystyle j_{\alpha}^{-1}((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);y)=j_{\alpha}^{-1}((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);y-1).

(S380)

Therefore,

\displaystyle r((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},N-1);0,\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i}=r((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);\,l((\mathbf{j}_{a},0);N-1,\mathbf{d})_{b},\mathbf{d})_{i}.

(S381)

This proves Eq. (
S314
).

IV
Analytical expressions for the Drude weight and specific heat for a fixed number of holes

In this section, we derive analytical expressions for the Drude weight and specific heat for a fixed number of holes
n_{h}
, i.e., with
n_{h}
fixed in the thermodynamic limit. We have also shown that
\sigma_{\mathrm{diff}}(\omega,T)=2\pi D(T)\,\delta(\omega)
; see the definition in Sec.
II.3
.

IV.1
Single-hole case

IV.1.1
Partition function

Based on the exact solutions for the energy spectrum derived in Sec.
III.1
, we now provide the analytical expression for the partition function with a single hole.
The partition function is defined as

\displaystyle Z=\text{Tr}\ (e^{-\beta\hat{H}})=\sum_{n}e^{-\beta E_{n}}.

(S382)

Explicitly, it reads

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}+\sum_{n_{\downarrow}=1}^{N-2}\frac{\binom{N-1}{n_{\downarrow}}}{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m_{\downarrow}=0}^{N-2}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-1)}\right)}

(S383)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}+\frac{2^{\,N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m_{\downarrow}=0}^{N-2}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-1)}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}+\frac{2^{\,N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m_{\downarrow}=0}^{N-2}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi((N-1)m+m_{\downarrow})}{N(N-1)}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}+\frac{2^{\,N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}\right)},

where the factor
2
in the first term arises because it accounts for the contribution from both sectors with
n_{\downarrow}=0
and
n_{\downarrow}=N-1
. These two sectors have the same energy spectrum, as one can be obtained from the other by flipping the spins of all fermions. In the second equality we have used the binomial identity
\sum_{n_{\downarrow}=1}^{N-2}\binom{N-1}{n_{\downarrow}}=\sum_{n_{\downarrow}=0}^{N-1}\binom{N-1}{n_{\downarrow}}-2=2^{N-1}-2
.
The large degeneracy originates from the fact that, in the

U\to\infty
limit, the Hubbard model reduces to the
t
-
J
model
with
J=0
, so that the antiferromagnetic superexchange vanishes and
only the charge degrees of freedom remain active. In the fourth equality, we define
q=(N-1)m+m_{\downarrow}
, so that
q=0,\cdots,N(N-1)-1
.

Using the Jacobi–Anger expansion
Colton et al. 1998
;
Cuyt et al. 2008

\displaystyle e^{-2\beta\cos\theta}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\,e^{il\theta}

\displaystyle=I_{0}(2\beta)+2\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\cos(l\theta),

(S384)

where
I_{l}(\cdot)
is the
l
-th modified Bessel function of the first kind,
the partition function is rewritten as

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle=2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\sum_{l=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\,e^{il\frac{2\pi m}{N}}+\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}\sum_{l=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\,e^{il\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{l=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\,\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{il\frac{2\pi m}{N}}+\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{l=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\,\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}e^{il\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}}

(S385)

where in the second equality we have simply exchanged the order of summation.
Since

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{il\frac{2\pi m}{N}}=\begin{cases}N,&l\in N\mathbb{Z},\\[4.0pt]
0,&l\notin N\mathbb{Z},\end{cases}

(S386)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}e^{il\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}}=\begin{cases}N(N-1),&l\in N(N-1)\mathbb{Z},\\[4.0pt]
0,&l\notin N(N-1)\mathbb{Z},\end{cases}

(S387)

The partition function is

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle=2N\sum_{p=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN}I_{pN}(2\beta)+N(N-1)\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{p=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN(N-1)}I_{pN(N-1)}(2\beta)

\displaystyle=2N\sum_{p=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN}I_{pN}(2\beta)+N(2^{N-1}-2)\sum_{p=-\infty}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN(N-1)}I_{pN(N-1)}(2\beta)

\displaystyle=2NI_{0}(2\beta)+4N\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN}I_{pN}(2\beta)+N(2^{N-1}-2)I_{0}(2\beta)+2N(2^{N-1}-2)\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN(N-1)}I_{pN(N-1)}(2\beta)

\displaystyle=N2^{N-1}I_{0}(2\beta)+4N\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN}I_{pN}(2\beta)+2N(2^{N-1}-2)\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN(N-1)}I_{pN(N-1)}(2\beta),

(S388)

where in the third equality, we have used
I_{-p}(2\beta)=I_{p}(2\beta)
. In the thermodynamic limit, the asymptotic expressions for
I_{pN}(2\beta)
and
I_{pN(N-1)}(2\beta)
are

\displaystyle I_{pN}(2\beta)

\displaystyle\sim\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi pN}}\left(\frac{e\beta}{pN}\right)^{pN},

(S389)

\displaystyle I_{pN(N-1)}(2\beta)

\displaystyle\sim\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi pN(N-1)}}\left(\frac{e\beta}{pN(N-1)}\right)^{pN(N-1)},

(S390)

which are exponentially suppressed. Therefore, the thermodynamic term, i.e., the leading term in
N
, for the partition function is given by
N2^{N-1}I_{0}(2\beta)
. Next, we consider the subleading correction in terms of
N
. When
p=1
, using the asymptotic forms shown in Eqs. (
S389
) and (
S390
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{2N\bigl(2^{N-1}-2\bigr)I_{N(N-1)}(2\beta)}{4NI_{N}(2\beta)}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bigl(2^{N-1}-2\bigr)I_{N(N-1)}(2\beta)}{2I_{N}(2\beta)}

\displaystyle\sim\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{2\sqrt{N-1}}\left(\frac{e\beta}{N(N-1)}\right)^{N(N-1)}\left(\frac{N}{e\beta}\right)^{N}.

(S391)

Taking the logarithm gives

\displaystyle\ln\!\left[\frac{\bigl(2^{N-1}-2\bigr)I_{N(N-1)}(2\beta)}{2I_{N}(2\beta)}\right]\sim N\ln 2-N(N-1)\ln\!\left(\frac{N(N-1)}{e\beta}\right)+N\ln\!\left(\frac{N}{e\beta}\right),

(S392)

whose leading term scales as
-2N^{2}\ln N
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{2N\bigl(2^{N-1}-2\bigr)I_{N(N-1)}(2\beta)}{4NI_{N}(2\beta)}\to 0,\qquad N\to\infty.

(S393)

Consequently, the subleading term is
4(-1)^{N}NI_{N}(2\beta)
.

In conclusion, in the thermodynamic limit, the partition function is

\displaystyle Z\approx N\,2^{\,N-1}\,I_{0}(2\beta)+4(-1)^{N}NI_{N}(2\beta),

(S394)

where
4(-1)^{N}NI_{N}(2\beta)
is the subleading term in
N
. Here, “
\approx
” indicates that higher-order terms in
N
have been neglected.

IV.1.2
Drude weight

In this section, we derive an analytical expression for the Drude weight in the single-hole sector. As discussed in Sec.
II.3
, only the Drude weight contributes to the optical conductivity.

For the present model,
due to
[\hat{J},\hat{H}]=0
,
the Drude weight is given by

\displaystyle D(T)=\frac{1}{2N}\langle-\hat{H}\rangle=-\frac{1}{2N}\frac{\text{Tr}\ (\hat{H}e^{-\beta\hat{H}})}{Z}.

(S395)

We therefore evaluate the trace
\text{Tr}\ (\hat{H}e^{-\beta\hat{H}})
.
Explicitly, it reads

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\ (\hat{H}e^{-\beta\hat{H}})

\displaystyle=2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)+\sum_{n_{\downarrow}=1}^{N-2}\frac{\binom{N-1}{n_{\downarrow}}}{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m_{\downarrow}=0}^{N-2}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-1)}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-1)}\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)+\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m_{\downarrow}=0}^{N-2}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-1)}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}+\frac{2\pi m_{\downarrow}}{N(N-1)}\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)+\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\sum_{m_{\downarrow}=0}^{N-2}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi((N-1)m+m_{\downarrow})}{N(N-1)}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi((N-1)m+m_{\downarrow})}{N(N-1)}\right)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)+\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}e^{-2\beta\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}\right)}2\cos\!\left(\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}\right).

(S396)

Applying the Jacobi–Anger expansion (see Eq. (
S384
)),
we obtain

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\ (\hat{H}e^{-\beta\hat{H}})

\displaystyle=2I_{0}(2\beta)\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}2\cos(\frac{2\pi m}{N})+4\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}2\cos(l\frac{2\pi m}{N})\cos(\frac{2\pi m}{N})

\displaystyle+\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}I_{0}(2\beta)\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}2\cos(\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})

\displaystyle+2\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}2\cos(l\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})\cos(\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})

\displaystyle=2I_{0}(2\beta)\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}2\cos(\frac{2\pi m}{N})+4\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\left(\cos((l+1)\frac{2\pi m}{N})+\cos((l-1)\frac{2\pi m}{N})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}I_{0}(2\beta)\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}2\cos(\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})

\displaystyle+2\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}\left(\cos((l+1)\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})+\cos((l-1)\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})\right)

\displaystyle=4\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\left(\cos((l+1)\frac{2\pi m}{N})+\cos((l-1)\frac{2\pi m}{N})\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{2^{N-1}-2}{N-1}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{l}I_{l}(2\beta)\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}\left(\cos((l+1)\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})+\cos((l-1)\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})\right).

(S397)

In the second equality, we have used

2\cos\theta\cos(l\theta)=\cos((l-1)\theta)+\cos((l+1)\theta)
. In the third equality, we have used
\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\cos(\frac{2\pi m}{N})=0
and
\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}\cos(\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)})=0
. Since

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\cos\!\left((l+1)\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)=\begin{cases}N,&l+1\in N\mathbb{Z},\\[4.0pt]
0,&l+1\notin N\mathbb{Z},\end{cases}

(S398)

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{N-1}\cos\!\left((l-1)\frac{2\pi m}{N}\right)=\begin{cases}N,&l-1\in N\mathbb{Z},\\[4.0pt]
0,&l-1\notin N\mathbb{Z},\end{cases}

(S399)

\displaystyle\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}\cos\!\left((l+1)\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}\right)=\begin{cases}N(N-1),&l+1\in N(N-1)\mathbb{Z},\\[4.0pt]
0,&l+1\notin N(N-1)\mathbb{Z},\end{cases}

(S400)

\displaystyle\sum_{q=0}^{N(N-1)-1}\cos\!\left((l-1)\frac{2\pi q}{N(N-1)}\right)=\begin{cases}N(N-1),&l-1\in N(N-1)\mathbb{Z},\\[4.0pt]
0,&l-1\notin N(N-1)\mathbb{Z},\end{cases}

(S401)

we obtain

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\ (\hat{H}e^{-\beta\hat{H}})

\displaystyle=4N\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN-1}I_{pN-1}(2\beta)+4N\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN+1}I_{pN+1}(2\beta)

\displaystyle+N(2^{N}-4)\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN(N-1)-1}I_{pN(N-1)-1}(2\beta)+N(2^{N}-4)\sum_{p=0}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN(N-1)+1}I_{pN(N-1)+1}(2\beta)

\displaystyle=-N2^{N}I_{1}(2\beta)+4N\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN-1}I_{pN-1}(2\beta)+4N\sum_{p=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{pN+1}I_{pN+1}(2\beta)
